A destination edit reaching unsent or dripping stages returns `409 landing_page_in_use` `{ affected, committed }` until resent with `confirm: true` (one query warns and gates, `lib/api/landing-page-impact.ts`); switching to slug is refused while an affected stage's brand has no landing host. No migration, no permission change. — docs updated: docs/07-conventions.md, docs/03-data-model.md, docs/04-features/registry.md, docs/CHANGELOG.md, spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-17-landing-page-edit-design.md

- 2026-09-17 — Phase 3 Task 3: `keitaro_stage_results`' conversion columns (checkouts/sales/revenue/payout_at_conversion) become a projection of the `conversion_events` ledger dated by `occurred_at` instead of the aggregate poll folding `conversions/log` dated by Keitaro's `datetime` — fixes bug 2 (a re-posted conversion counted on two stage-days, +$100). New `lib/keitaro/stage-day-conversions.ts` (`syncStageDayConversions`, `changedLedgerStageIds`); `lib/keitaro/poll.ts` no longer fetches `conversions/log` (`applyConversionRowToAggregate` deleted, `mirrorStageCountersFromResults` exported); `/api/keitaro/poll` runs the projection after an `ok` ledger ingest, scoped to the click window's stages ∪ ledger rows changed in the last 30 min. One-shot repair: `scripts/resync-stage-day-conversions.ts` (dry-run by default; not yet applied to prod — migrations 0181/0182 are still behind the Phase 3 STOP gate). Semantics unchanged at this step (sales = lead+sale+rejected, checkouts = lead). — docs updated: docs/04-features/keitaro-poll.md, docs/04-features/conversion-events.md, docs/07-conventions.md, docs/CHANGELOG.md

- 2026-09-17 — Phase 3 Task 2 review fixes: the operator API's `POOLS_DEFINITION` now states the ledger meaning of *converted* (a purchase-type conversion event in status pending or approved; a rejected conversion or a registration does NOT count) instead of "a tracker conversion on this offer's messages"; `purchasedClause()` is parenthesised so an `OR` call site cannot rebind its `AND`; the stale `sale_status IN ('lead','sale')` definitions in `lib/validators/segment-rule-types.ts`, `lib/campaign-tier.ts`, `lib/drip/lifecycle-sweep.ts` and docs/07-conventions.md are corrected. New `scripts/test-segment-purchase-rules-db.ts` runs the REAL `buildSegmentAudienceClause` for all three `made_purchase*` rules plus `computeAudiencePools` against ONE-SIDED fixtures (ledger-only / legacy-only / rejected / registration / cross-brand / cross-offer / cross-org) — the switched rules had never executed anywhere; the tier and drip suites gained the same controls (34 / 15 / 29 checks, 0 failed on camman-v2; 17, 3 and 5 of them go red against the pre-ledger readers). `conversion_events` + `event_types` added to the four lane/split leak guards. No migration, no permission change, no behaviour change beyond the definition text. — docs updated: docs/07-conventions.md, docs/CHANGELOG.md

- 2026-09-17 — Phase 3 Task 3 review fix C1: the stage-day projection can no longer zero what the ledger has not covered. `syncStageDayConversions` REFUSES when `conversion_events` holds no stage-attributed row (`refused: "empty_ledger"`, the refusal the resync script already carried) and bounds its zeroing UPDATE by a PER-STAGE coverage floor (a stage-day is zeroable only on or after that stage's earliest ledger conversion) — without it the first prod tick would have zeroed months of real sales/revenue for every stage with a recent click, because the live ingest window is only 7 ET days. `coverageFloor` + `refused` are returned and surfaced in the poll response. Review fix I2: `mirrorStageCountersFromResults` gains `exactCheckoutClicks`, which the projection passes, so a zeroed day can pull `campaign_stages.checkout_click_count` DOWN (the positive-only guard made the non-monotonic projection's corrections invisible to the campaign page and the creatives metrics cache); `click_count`/`sales_payout_each` keep the guard, `sales_count` is untouched, and the mirror now throws instead of swallowing inside a caller's transaction. Minors: `payout_at_conversion` joins the upsert's distinctness guard so a stale/NULL payout is repaired, the zeroing resets `pending_revenue` (0182) too, `stagesInScope` reports the scope it was GIVEN, the changed-stage list is capped at 2000 ids, the stale `epc`-column-drop comment is gone, and the resync dry run applies the same coverage floor `--apply` does. Also corrected: docs/07-conventions.md still described the deleted `agg.sales += 1` fold in `lib/keitaro/poll.ts` as current, and `lib/sale-attribution.ts` claimed a Task 7 script exists. Checks: `scripts/test-stage-day-conversions.ts` 32/0 on camman-v2 (the three coverage bounds go red against the pre-fix module while the bug-2 correction stays green). No migration, no permission change. ⚠️ Ordering for Task 8: Phase 1's `scripts/backfill-conversion-events.ts --apply` on prod, and a human reading the resync dry run, BOTH precede deploying this code. — docs updated: docs/04-features/conversion-events.md, docs/04-features/keitaro-poll.md, docs/07-conventions.md, docs/CHANGELOG.md
- 2026-09-17 — Phase 3 Task 3 review fix I3: the stage-day projection is resumable and pages when it fails. Discovery was a fixed 30-minute `conversion_events.updated_at` lookback with no cursor, so ~6 consecutive failed ticks (or route kills after the ingest commits) stranded changed stage-days forever with no alert. `discoverChangedLedgerStages` + `advanceProjectionWatermark` now keep a watermark in `cron_locks.watermark` under `job_name = 'conversion-stage-day-projection'` (the column `propagate-clickers` already uses; not a lease): the window is `[min(watermark − 5 min, now − 30 min), now]`, so the lookback is only the FLOOR and an older watermark EXTENDS the window back, and `runStageDayProjection` advances the cursor ONLY after a projection that neither threw nor refused. A capped run (2000 stage ids, oldest change first) advances only as far as the ids it kept and reports `truncated`. New latched Tier-2 alert `conversion_events:projection_failed` (`lib/conversions/monitor.ts`): fires when the projection throws or refuses (empty ledger), clears on the next successful run, evaluated on the CRON path only and only when the projection actually ran — a skipped projection keeps getting no decision, since the ingest's own `fetch_failed` alert covers that tick. The poll response's `stage_day_conversions` now carries `watermarkTo` and the `discovery` window. Checks: `scripts/test-stage-day-conversions.ts` 39/0 on camman-v2 (first run, out-of-window, an old watermark extending the window, the cap, advance-only-on-success, no advance on a refusal, plus a source guard on the route's cron-only alert), `scripts/test-conversion-monitor.ts` 44/0 (J1-J5), `scripts/test-conversion-monitor-db.ts` 48/0 (C1-C5 latch). No migration, no permission change. — docs updated: docs/04-features/conversion-events.md, docs/04-features/keitaro-poll.md, docs/07-conventions.md, docs/CHANGELOG.md

- 2026-09-18 — Phase 3 Task 4 RE-REVIEW fixes. (1) The `F5` source guard in `scripts/test-p3-task4-reader-switch-db.ts` could never fail: its negative needle spanned a line break and this checkout is CRLF, so restoring the activity cell's old inline style map verbatim kept it GREEN. It is now a regex over whitespace-collapsed source, keyed on tells only a re-inlined copy carries; proven red by restoring that map, then restored `cmp`-clean. The other five source greps were audited (all single-line; each negated needle is a string the pre-switch code really contained) — 1 defect found, 1 fixed. (2) The `unmapped` fixture was over-determined (both `event_type_id` and `status` NULL), so a reader keyed on status alone passed it by accident; the guard now carries BOTH shapes the ingest produces — `unmapped_status_only` (status-only mapping rule: no event type, status `approved`) and `mapped_status_null` (a mapped purchase type whose status did not map, reachable because the upsert keeps `event_type_id` sticky while `status` takes `excluded.status`) — and asserts neither is a purchase, in approved or pending revenue, a `sale`-weight candidate, or rescued. Check A3 was rewritten to discriminate (it read 0/0 under the old code too). (3) `conversionBadgeClass` returned NO class for a mapped type with a NULL status; it now returns the new `CONVERSION_STATUS_UNKNOWN_STYLE` (neutral + ring, never a purchase colour), which also catches any unknown future status. (4) An uncounted amount now renders as `· $55.00 uncounted` via the shared `conversionAmountLabel()`, used by both the badge and its tooltip ($0 and rejected suppressions unchanged). (5) `scripts/verify-counted-clickers.ts`'s comment no longer claims total independence — the rescue half repeats the cache's own join shape; the real asymmetry (this check passes no `window`, the incremental pass does) is now what it says. Check unchanged. (6) `hourlyEtRange`'s second, non-hourly caller and the partner report's test-unreachable `restrict` bound are named in comments rather than re-engineered. Checks: `scripts/test-p3-task4-reader-switch-db.ts` 76/0 on camman-v2 (was 62/0); red proofs: F5 red with the old map restored, 14 red incl. the shape-(b) assertions against a status-only reader, 7 red incl. the NULL-status and badge-fallback assertions against a not-rejected reader; all mutations restored byte-identically (`cmp` + md5). `scripts/test-ledger-predicates.ts` 15/0, unchanged. No migration, no permission change, no query-semantics change. — docs updated: docs/04-features/conversion-events.md, docs/05-flows.md, docs/07-conventions.md, docs/CHANGELOG.md

- 2026-09-18 — Phase 4 Task 1: migration 0184 widens `campaign_stages_behavioral_lane_check` to admit `behavioral_tier = 3` (the future Registered lane) while still refusing 4 (the purchased tier stays an EXIT, not a lane). Purely permissive — no column change, no backfill (every stored tier is 0/1/2/NULL; prod holds zero rows at 3 across `campaign_stages`/`report_stage_hour`/`report_group_hour`). `db/schema.ts`'s CHECK and its comment block mirror the new set. Near-inert for the application (nothing creates a tier-3 lane yet), but NOT inert for the test suite: `scripts/test-behavioral-split.ts`'s `CHECK rejects tier=3` assertion is now false under the new CHECK, so it moves to assert `CHECK rejects tier=4` in the same commit — green end to end. New `scripts/test-registered-lane-tier-db.ts` (C1-C8, camman-v2 only) proves the widening: red before the migration deploys (C1/C5 fail — the CHECK still says `(0,1,2)` and a tier-3 insert is rejected), green after (8/8). — docs updated: docs/03-data-model.md, docs/CHANGELOG.md

> When you change behavior that a doc describes, update the doc **and** add an entry here in the same PR (Part B rule).
